GCW — Pay-per-view

Dangerous Games 2009, 28 October 2009

28 October 2009
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Dangerous Games 2009 Land Shark Stadium — Miami, FL ---- Introduction The GCW logo flashes on the screen amidst complete silence. LAST YEAR... A deep, narrator-esque voice breaks the silence as a collage of superstar portraits flash across the screen. Vivica J. Valentine, Jake Reed, Dan "The Bulldozer" Black, Chris Storm, Jacob McKail, Chris Bagwell, Axel Action, Zacharia Taylor, Digital Mortality... THIRTY SUPERSTARS ENGAGED IN THE MOST PROLIFIC BATTLE IN RECENT HISTORY The collage continues with Clyde Fox, Markus Stone, Scott Stevens, Jordan White, Kiljaden Kincaid, Brad Jackson, Tyson DeBough, Garbage Bag Johnny, and Andy Murray. BUT ONLY ONE STOOD ABOVE THEM ALL The collage of past and present superstars fades into a single silhouette. Massive in size, the silhouette slowly comes into focus to reveal last year's winner, LeStatt Knight, standing tall inside the ring at the conclusion of Dangerous Games 2008. THIS YEAR... The collage ensues - but this time with the addition of Cade Carson, Barney Green, and James Mercier THE SUPERSTARS ARE STRONGER Jorge Samuelsson, Bishop Steele, Aaron Nothings... QUICKER SurReal, CJ Woods, Cheap Labor... MORE DARING M3, Kris Michaels, Lantlas Anduril, Lori Equinox... AND JUST SIMPLY MORE BIZARRE James von Doom. DANGEROUS GAMES 2009 The collage of superstars fade into the Dangerous Games logo set a cast a panoramic shot of the Landshark Stadium in Miami, Florida. Ninety thousand screams fans are on their feet as the cameras pan around, highlighting the gorgeous Miami skyline. Pyrotechnics light the air and the stage and serve as a cue for the most cunning commentating duo in the industry. James Bryan: With over ninety thousand people in attendance, we are LIVEEEEEEEE from Landshark Stadium in the beautiful Miami, Florida. We are LIVE on pay-per-view. And by God, we are LIVE at DANGEROUS GAMES 2009!! Good evening ladies and gentlemen, my name is James Bryan and I'd like to cordially welcome you to one of the most thrilling events in the business. I'm joined by my always apprehensible colleague, David Yale. David Yale: Maaaaannnn, JB, am I thrilled for the start of this one. You know how much I love the big 30-man battle royal. I've waited all year for tonight! Bryan: Tonight will undoubtedly go down in the record books as one of the greatest Dangerous Games of all time, Dave. The field for the big thirty man rumble is wide open with literally no indication of a favorite. Yale: Last year, there was a clear group of individuals who stood out of the pack. I could think of about 6 off the top of my head. But this year, all those same guys from last year have returned and they're better than ever. To be honest, I could think of about fifteen different possible outcomes right now. Bryan: Any favorite to take the whole thing, Dave? Yale: It's just such a hard choice, JB. I can't reason an answer but what I can do is tell you who I'd like to see take the cake tonight - and that's Tyson DeBough. Tyson is currently the United States Champion and the sole holder of the Tag Team Championship titles, with Brian Fisher being on injury. The guy has been underrated his whole career and I think he's more than capable of getting the job done. Bryan: DeBough is most certainly a valid choice. He's done quite a lot over the last several months and despite losing to Axel Action at Warpath, he's been relatively unstoppable. But my personal favorite has to be Clyde Fox. That kid has come so unbelievably far since joining the company a few years ago and I'd love to see him grab the back to back Rampage and Dangerous Games wins. Yale: That's something I don't think has ever been done, JB. Bryan: If there's one guy that's hot right now - it's got to be Clyde Fox. If not for anything else - Clyde deserves it. He really does. Yale: Well JB, we've still got a long journey before we reach the main event. Not that the journey is a bad one - no, no - it's going to keep you on the edge of your seat the whole way. Bryan: You're absolutely right, Dave. Tonight, we'll also see the returning CJ Woods battle newcomer and current Television Champion Cade Carson in a singles contest. Yale: Carson has made quite an impact since joining the GCW. He toppled Lucas Quinton in his second match and has progressed quite nicely in mixed tag matches. However you cannot discount the statement CJ Woods made in GCW before his brief leave of absence. CJ is back, JB, and he wants to make sure everyone is aware. Bryan: Woods is a kid with a lot of heart and a helluva lot of talent. This title shot has been a long way in the making for him. We're also going to be naming a new American Dream winner tonight during a triple threat match between Cheap Labor, Mysterious Masked Mexican, and Dan Black. Yale: An interesting fact, JB - Dan Black has never been in an American Dream match. And the most ironic part of that is he's defeated just about every guy who participated in the last two American Dream matches! Bryan: Black has been sitting on the brink of the division for some time and will finally get his shot as the American Dream was made vacant with the abrupt departure of Mark in the Dark. But one cannot ignore the impact Mysterious Masked Mexican has made in such a short time. M3 has wrapped a couple of victorious under his belt, as well, and has the momentum rolling clearly in his favor. Yale: Or even Cheap Labor, who recently signed from GCW affiliate JUST Wrestling. Cheap Labor hasn't been so impressive in GCW thus far, but his history in JUST says wonders for what he can do in the ring. Bryan: Our American Dream winner won't have to go far to see who he'll be hunting for as Tyson DeBough defends his United States Championship belt in a rematch against Axel Action, who defeated DeBough at Warpath. Yale: DeBough was all set for a monster battle with Mark in the Dark until Zacharia Taylor accidentally shattered his collar bone in a match on Worldwide. Axel stepped in to defend the team of Andy Murray, Clyde Fox, and Zacharia Taylor, and subsequently was tossed into a match with DeBough to "teach him a lesson." Never in a million years did anyone in the Establishment expect Axel to pull off a win. Bryan: Although Lantlas Anduril defeated Dan Black at Warpath to be named the number one contender, Axel's victory over DeBough took precedence and that's why we're here tonight! After tonight, Lantlas Anduril resumes his role as number one contender. Yale: You know what's really wild, JB? Should DeBough be victorious, he's not only going to know who the number one contender for the US title is and who the American Dream holder is - he's also going to find out which tag team will be on the hunt for his tag team titles! Bryan: You're absolutely right! With Brian Fisher on the shelf, Tyson DeBough has informed the officials that he wishes to defend the belts by himself until official word on Brian's injury comes back. At Warpath, we had the makings of a tag team contest where the winning foursome would battle to become the number one contenders. However it was Mortifera that absolutely demolished all three other teams. Since Warpath, Team PROjection, Holy Hell, and December Revolution have all been released from their contracts. This left Mortifera as the sole tag team in the division. Yale: Enter James von Doom! Von Doom returns with the Church of the Unholy and his newest minion, Count von MacAbre. Who would've ever thought we'd see James von Doom back in a GCW ring? Bryan: Not me, Dave. Not in this lifetime, at least. But I suppose that's the mystique about von Doom's ways. He always manages to get himself involved. Now he's lined up in a number one contenders match for the Tag Titles! Yale: Not without a hell of a fight, though. Bryan: I think that phrase is better suited for the Falls Count Anywhere match between Vivica J. Valentine and the Man in Black - or should I say Digital Mortality! After stalking Vivica for months and stalking the GCW for over a year, the Man in Black was finally revealed as former United States champion Digital Mortality. Yale: I've always wondered what happened to Digital Mortality. He just disappeared one day. But this explains it all. He's been lurking in the shadows all along, waiting for the right moment to strike. Bryan: We've seen Vivica underestimated time and again. What people forget is that she's the Fearless Phenom and thrives in situations like these. I'm sure everyone remembers her battle with Chris Bagwell back at NC-17. Valentine went in there without a shadow of a chance and ended up forcing Bagwell to quit. On the contrary, you have to imagine that Digital Mortality is looking to get himself in the title hunt and what better way to do so than by defeating a former champion! Yale: And then comes our first of two main events, JB. The "Scottish King of Cool" Andy Murray takes on a man who once tried to bury this very company, Jason O'Neil. Bryan: In President Steven Caldera's latest act of desperation, he's recruited a man he hasn't seen eyes to eyes with in over 7 years - the very same man who tried to take everything Caldera had. I must admit that I'm still in shock that Caldera has brought in Jason O'Neil to try and capture the World Championship from Andy Murray. Yale: Nothing that President Caldera does at this point should surprise you. Remember, this is the same guy who dumped LeStatt Knight...who manipulated Jay Terror...who got Banned & Exiled~! to sell out. Steven Caldera has a history of doing the impossible in order to maintain full control over his belts. Bryan: I suppose your right. I guess deep down, I worry that Andy Murray may not be able to walk away from this one with the belt. O'Neil has a very decorated past and showed us last week that the term "ring rust" isn't in his vocabulary. After his near-loss to Chris Bagwell, Andy Murray just hasn't been the same guy. Yale: I honestly believe Andy's days as champion are numbered. Steven Caldera would not entrust his main event of one of his biggest events of the year in a man that he didn't believe could dismantle Murray. And don't forget, Jason O'Neil is a Hall of Famer. What are the chances that Andy can topple two Hall of Famers in less than six months? Bryan: The odds are certainly in O'Neil's favor but I'm sure the millions around the world are in Andy's corner! Yale: I guess tonight is the true test of the old "twelfth man" theory. Bryan: Ladies and gentlemen, we've made it. Dangerous Games 2009 is here and there's no sense in waiting any longer. Let's get this show started! ---- Dangerous Predictions: 2 The camera heads backstage once again, where the smug face of The Jhub immediately fills the screen. The crowd explodes at the unexpected sight, and the camera slowly zooms out to reveal the Hall of Famer, wearing his old System-X t-shirt, standing next to a somewhat annoyed looking 10-time Hardcore Champion Toad and a grinning Kevin Jacobs. Toad: I thought we agreed to share that close-up. The Jhub: Are you kidding me? There's no way I'm letting that face on television. Jacobs: Uh, guys... Toad: What the hell are you talking about? Nobody came here to look at your ugly Persian face. The Jhub: Me? Remember the time Caldera made you wrestle with a bag over your head, and the ratings immediately shot up five points because "that ugly troll thing was gone?" Toad: Ha! Remember the time we went to that show in Tijuana, and they had to call it off because the donkey couldn't get turned on with you in the room? Jacobs: Uhh, we're here with System-X, The Jhub and Toad, two former staples of the big Dangerous Games match. Guys, some major names have come back tonight to check out this year's Dangerous Games, and we're looking for some predictions. Guys, who do you think will be headlining NC-17 this year? The Jhub: Well, you know, we've talked about this a lot in recent weeks. Toad: It's true. It's kept us up nights. The Jhub: Yeah, we've really been laboring over this. We've studied the field, we've gone over video, we've read all the scouting reports, we've drank a lot of beer, we've done our homework. Toad: We really have broken this down very scientifically. The Jhub: And after weeks and weeks of painstaking research, endless hours in the lab and quite a few sleepless nights, System-X has reached an irrefutable conclusion about this year's Dangerous Games match. Reaching into his pocket The Jhub produces a pair of glasses and a piece of white paper. Sliding the glasses on he clears this throat and examines the paper with a raised eyebrow. The Jhub: Every single wrestler in this year's match sucks ass. Toad: Sucks it hard. Jacobs: I'm sorry? The Jhub: You heard me, Kevin Jacobs. Every single one of them. They all suck. It's all just different shades of crap. Jacobs: What about Clyde Fox? The Jhub: Sucks. Jacobs: Zacharia Taylor? Toad: Sucks. Jacobs: Vivica J. Valentine? The Jhub: Sucks. Toad: We actually tested that theory a number of times. The Jhub: But don't you worry your little combover off, Kevin. Because we've come to the conclusion that there's only one way to save this sorry-ass event. Tonight, System-X will ride high again by entering the Dangerous Games match! The crowd roars at this announcement, as Toad reaches off camera and produces his ever-present baseball bat. Jacobs: The two of you are actually going to be IN the match tonight? Toad: You heard the jackass, jackass. You think you're gonna keep the two biggest stars in the world out of this match? The Jhub: Tonight we're going to show these new kids how Dangerous Games really works. And when it's all over, The Jhub will be back in the main event with the World Title around his waist! The fans explode, but Toad flashes a quizzical look. Toad: Whoa, whoa, whoa, I thought we agreed that I would win Dangerous Games. You've won the title two times already, I think it's my turn. The Jhub: You? With a face like yours they'd be better off giving the title to Courtney Love's vagina. Toad: Yeah, well, at least I never lost to Shane Lane. The Jhub opens his mouth to respond, but nothing comes. The Jhub: Well played. With that, System-X departs, Toad still gripping his bat in anticipation. Bryan: Wow! The Hall of Famer and the Hardcore Legend have entered themselves in this year's Dangerous Games match! Yale: Oh great, this should be fun... You know I'm not sure we've seen Rikki Roxx since the last time these guys were around. Are we even sure he's still alive? Bryan: No, actually... But at any rate, this year's Dangerous Games match just got a whole lot more competitive! System-X returns to the ring for Dangerous Games tonight! ---- CJ Woods vs. Cade Carson Andrews: The following contest is scheduled for one fall and is for the GCW Television Title! Bryan: It's time to kick off Dangerous Games' matches with an exciting Television title match between CJ Woods and Cade Carson! Yale: It sure smells like that time. Just listen to the crowd - no one's even come out yet! Bryan: Mostly in part for CJ Woods I'd wager, the guy's pulling fan-pools the size of Andy Murray's gathering! Yale: That's true. You have to give credit where credit is due though. Cade Carson is the current champion after defeating Lucas Quinton at Worldwide 118 and he's looked impressive ever since. Bryan: But remember what happened at the end of that one. CJ Woods made his return directly after the match sending Carson down with the Timbertoppler. Yale: Hence why we have this match tonight at one of GCW's premier events - Dangerous Games. It looks like it's about time to get under way, let's go down to ringside with Andrews for the official introductions. "Swan Dive" by (hed)PE hits the sound system. I climb, hand over hand, closing my eyes Too scared to look down I climb, hand over hand Putting distance between I and I and the ground CJ Woods emerges from the back in full cigarette smoke plumage. Cigarette in mouth and a bottle of water in one hand. I get to the top I stand on the edge I look to the sky, and say all my prayers What could be better than a swan dive into the asphalt I don't know, nothing can be better than a swan dive into the asphalt Andrews: Introducing first...the challenger hailing from New York City, New York. He weighs in at two hundred and eighty-nine pounds....THE NEW YORK LUMBERJACK....CJJJJJ.....WOOOOOOOODDDDSSSSS!!! The chorus kicks in and he jogs down to the ring. He high fives fans on his way down and slides under the bottom rope into the ring. Climbs the nearest turnbuckle to finish his cigarette and flicks it away before taking a swig of water. then he shakes the water up like it were a bottle of champagne, objective to soak himself and the front row in water. Andrews: And his opponent... The lights go out, descending the entire arena into darkness. A moment passes, then another, before the opening lyrics of "Cry Little Sister" play over the arena speakers, a shrill noise cutting through the darkness like a knife. Suddenly, the lights begin to strobe as a figure emerges from the back, his white full-length robe like a ceremonial gown covering his body, his face hidden underneath the folds of his hood. He walks with purpose down the aisle with the TV title wrapped around his waist, ignoring the fans as he passes them by on his way to the ring. His eyes remain focused on the ring, a pair of dark brown orbs that appear almost reddish in the eerie light. Andrews: Hailing from Detroit, Michigan and weighing in at two hundred and thirty-one pounds....He is your GCW TELEVISION CHAMPION.......THE TAINTED ONE.....CAAAADDEEEE.....CAAARRRRSSOOOOOOONNNNN!!! Sliding under the bottom rope, he makes his way to his corner, where he stands feet wide apart, arms down by his side. Suddenly, he lifts his arms in the air, throwing his head back in the process. The hood comes off; revealing a shaven head marked by ritual tattoos, including a red cross that traverses the top of his skull, from the beginning of what would be his hairline to the back of his skull. Likewise, when the robe is removed, his entire body is covered in similar markings. Lowering himself to his knees, he places the belt before him on the mat, brings his hands together, then, bowing his head, he closes his eyes and utters something silently, then touches his hand to four corners of his body -- the sign of the cross -- before opening his eyes, and standing to his feet. Bryan: And with that, it's time to get the first match of Dangerous Games underway! DING! DING! DING! Yale: Not just any match either, it's a title match for one of the most highly contested belts in GCW history. Great names have held that belt on their way to greatness and I'm sure that both of these men will be on their way up as soon as they move along in their careers. Bryan: That's right. Everyone who's been anyone in GCW has held that television title for a stint in their career. Carson and Woods both circle each other up feeling the other out as their eyes lock across the mat with fiery glances. Both shoot confidence with their glare and collectively, they're both surprised by the readiness they each have. Making the first move is the champion himself who steps inward extending his arms in an inward hook. He grabs CJ around the head and delivers a knee strike upward to his stomach. Woods catches the knee however and shoves Carson onto his back. Bryan: Good counter by Woods, let's see what he has in mind now. Yale: He's springing off the ropes now and it looks like...yes, there's an elbow drop across the chest of Carson. Bryan: Working the midsection already! The fans erupt at the counter and elbow already backing him up completely. He uses their cheers as food and allows Carson to get to his feet. Woods takes a sprint toward Cade and looks for a closeline from hell. Cade falls to the closeline giving another huge pop to the crowd and an exuberant fist pump from Woods. Carson stands again and the two superstars wrap up in a grapple. Woods elbows Cade in the side of the head and tucks his head under his arm. Yale: Looking for a DDT here possibly. Bryan: Woods lifts Carson off the mat and there it is - a huge lifting DDT! That's going to rattle the champs brain for a while. Yale: Easily. Woods is looking strong so far and you can't help but wonder where Carson's head is at. He's being stuffed on every opportunity already; could this be a sign of things to come? Bryan: I'm not sure. All I know is that Woods is looking for a pinfall already! ...1! ...2! ...KICKOUT! Yale: Cameron Wrigley with the count but that DDT wasn't enough to finish things this early. Woods thinks nothing of the pinfall and picks up Carson. He grapples on him once again and this time drops to his own knees executing a fireman's carry. Cade flips onto his back and CJ grabs him around the head as he tries to sit back up. He punches him in the temple before locking in a solid choke from behind. Bryan: Working his technical game now. Woods has a little bit of history with submissions in his training so we'll see how far it takes him tonight. Yale: He has the hold locked in fairly tight right now. Carson won't give up the neck though and is using his chin to fend off CJ's arm. Woods continues to struggle for the advantage on the choke but Carson is relentless shvoing his chin deep into his own chest to avoid it. Cutting his losses, Woods decides to stand bringing Carson with him. Now from behind, Woods attempts to lift Carson onto his shoulders. Cade has other plans though and uses his elbows to swim out of his grasp. He charges the ropes and springs back at Woods with a running missle dropkick. Bryan: Great dropkick by Carson, that could be the spark his engine was looking for. Yale: Indeed. Woods is back to his feet but Carson is keeping on him with a flurry of punches. Cade Carson is definitely not the person Woods wants to be boxing with right now. Bryan: Ever! He's the kind of guy that puts lights out before you can fend it off. Carson corners Woods against the turnbuckle pads and lowers his shoulder pushing all of his weight into CJ Woods' stomach. Woods doubles over and Carson immediately jumps to the second rope beside him. He hops into the air off a springboard and lifts his leg as high as possible delivering a swift roundhouse across the nose of Woods. He grabs his face without hesitation and makes sure he still has a nose. Carson continues his furious strikes as he comes around with a spinning roundhouse. Woods falls to the mat on his backside seated appropriately against the buckles. Yale: Carson uses his boot now to choke Woods! Listen to this crowd too! BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!! Bryan: They hate to see their hero drowning and that's exactly what's going on. He can't breathe with that leather against his esophagus. Yale: Cameron Wrigley is asking him to break it up, looks like Woods will get some reprieve. Woods only gets a few seconds of rest as Carson leans in to pick him up. He grabs him around the head but as he does Woods uses all of his weight and lifts his legs to perform a jawbreaker. Carson catches it and instead of falling on his backside he lands on his stomach crushing Woods instead. His head bounces off the turnbuckle pads but dazes him momentarily. Carson tries for the pin. ...1! ...2! ...FOOT ON ROPE! Bryan: Good sense of ring awareness this by Woods, he understands his surroundings so well. Yale: It's only natural. He's been around for a while and knows where he is at all times. Even if he is underneath his opponent. Bryan: Must be one of the qualities Andy Murray likes about him. Yale: That and the fact that he calls him cool. Carson slams his fist in rage, already frustrated by the lack of pinfall. He gets back to his feet with Woods in tow and wraps him up for an abdominal stretch in the center of the ring. He torques CJ Wood's body against its natural flow and holds him in place with his arms and legs. Woods bellows at first feeling the stretch fully but maintains his composure and listens to the crowd. CJ! CJ! CJ! CJ! Bryan: Hearing that can do wonders when in this position. I'm sure he's hulking up right now in his mind. Yale: Yep, I can see the green already! Woods begins to tap his foot but Carson quickly shuts him up by twisting harder and bending more. Unfortunately for Carson, the twist works against him and Woods manages to use his free hand to take control of his ankle. Yelling for hope, Woods fights Carson's power and slips out of his hold turning it into an ankle lock of his own. Carson falls on his face and Woods gathers his leg between his arms applying maximum pressure. Bryan: Great reversal by CJ Woods there, I didn't expect it from him. Yale: He's a technical fighter JB. He has the ability to pull out some rare transitions when given the right opportunity. Bryan: I can see that. Take a look at Carson's face in that lock. Yale: It's hunger in those eyes. The guy loves to win which is what brought him here in the first place. Now it seems to be working against him. Bryan: Woods continues to hold that lock in tight, it's a beautiful move when done correctly like this. Yale: Exactly. He has great leverage on him right now; the only thing missing would be the leg scissor around his entire leg. Carson struggles and grabs his hair with his hands. He drops his face into the mat shouting for a minute. He wants to tap, he can feel it in his gut. But he avoids it at all costs and instead focuses his time and energy in crawling ten extra feet to the ropes. Before attempting it though, he uses his free leg to kick wildly. Woods snuffs the attack and gets on his knee while holding the lock perfectly. Cade Carson understands what must be done and digs down deep grabbing all the will he can muster. One arms length at a time, he makes his way across the ring to the bottom ropes. Yale: It's a slow crawl but if he can keep it up then he can escape this soon. Bryan: Slow and steady wins the race they say. You can tell that it's going to leave some damage though. Yale: Exactly. He's going to be limping if Woods has anything to do with it. TAP OUT! TAP OUT! TAP OUT! Carson ignores the general consensus floating around the arena and continues on his march. Only inches away now, he can nearly feel the rope on his palm. Woods throws a curveball into the mix though and releases the ankle. He shoots his body upward at Carson's body mounting him. He elbows him once and then lifts his legs and midsection into the air in a handstand-esque stance. Quickly, he allows his weight to fall again crashing his knees onto the small back of Carson. Cade cringes in pain and Woods rolls him over. ...1! ...2! ...THR-KICKOUT! Bryan: CJ Woods just did what I didn't see coming and actually broke his own ankle lock for a knee drop. Yale: I don't think anyone saw that coming! The guy is shifty but obviously has a plan in mind. Woods stands while Carson remains on the mat. CJ takes a running start at the ropes and returns with a leaping somersault senton splash over Cade's chest. The move sends CJ outside of the ring and onto the floor mats though. Bryan: He calls that the wrecking ball and it did some damage to both of them. Yale: I don't think he realized how close they were to the ropes to be honest. Good thing he landed on his knees out there. Woods gathers himself while Carson tries to get back to his feet. While outside, CJ Woods takes in the excitement and walks to the barricade to slap high fives with the front row people. They welcome him whole heartedly and he allows a thick smile to cross his lips before running back to the ring and sliding inside. Cade Carson leans on the ropes and shakes off the cobwebs before facing a highly energized CJ Woods. Yale: Look at the happiness CJ is selling! I don't know how Carson's going to avoid buying a smile or two. Bryan: If Carson has anything left you mean. He looks a little beaten up out there. Woods makes his way toward Carson and tries for a grapple but Cade Carson uses his brawling technique to his advantage delivering a heavy right hook to CJ's cheek. Woods shakes it off but finds another punch, this time in his gut. Woods nurses it for a second while Carson finds a second wind and throws his body at him. The takedown is successful and Carson mounts his stomach giving him a chance to shine with left and right punches galore. Carson stops his strikes as he feels CJ hold still for a moment. In a second, Cade gets back to his feet and stands beside Woods. Gathering all the strength he has, he tucks his body and flips backwards landing a flashy moonsault into a pinfall. ...1! ...2! ...THR-KICKOUT! Bryan: I don't know where he got that surge of energy but it was a good use of it. Right across the stomach of Woods! Yale: It was a random surge but a much needed one. If he didn't get that soon then surely things were going to go downhill. Carson continues on the offense and lifts Woods off the mat, obvious frustration clouds his eyes. He turns Woods so his back meets his stomach and flings him upwards for a slow lift German suplex. The stall adds to the damage done to Woods's neck and leaves him lying flat on the mat in the center of the ring. Carson lifts Woods up so he's on his knees and from there he bends over grabbing his head and neck. Fluidly, he swings his neck and body around for a swinging neckbreaker across the mat. He covers once again for the pin. ...1! ...2! ...THRE-KICKOUT! Yale: That was another close one by our champion here. He used that signiture neck swing but it still wasn't enough to take down the New York Lumberjack. Bryan: It was enough to anger him even more though. We've seen this in his past matches where he begins to become unstable. Carson hates to have a match linger any longer than he feels and I think this one is going overtime in his mind. Yale: I wonder if he'll use it for good or bad. Bryan: If history tells us anything, it's that it can go either way. Mostly though he's been able to channel it right - hence why he owns the belt currently. Carson grabs Woods by the hair again tearing him to his feet. Cade strikes him toward the side ropes and manages to corner him between himself and the apron. Looking to send him over, Woods has other plans and ducks. He grabs behind Carson's knees and flips him over his back onto the mats below smacking with a hefty thud. Bryan: Great counter for Woods and now he's back in charge from inside the ring. Yale: But look at this, I think he's looking to do something. VAULT! VAULT! VAULT! VAULT! He entertains the fans by clapping through the pain in his body. The buy into him even more bringing him to a new high. As Carson begins to get back to his feet, Woods grabs hold of the ropes and takes a deep breath. Yale: He's going to vault over at Carson JB! Bryan: Woods grabs the ropes and there he goes! Springing over top he's soaring straight for the midsection of Carson with that crossbody splash! Yale: And listen to this ovation! The entire arena is standing! Woods and Carson both roll off one another and lie motionless on the mats. Fans hang over the barricades past the security force to get a good look at their hero. Inside the ring, the referee initiates his count. ...1! ...2! ...3! ...4! Woods makes the first move and begins to gather his senses. He crawls to Carson who has a hard time finding himself. ...5! Woods makes his way back to his feet with the wind of the crowd nearly knocking him forward. CJ WOODS! CJ WOODS! CJ WOODS! CJ WOODS! ...6! Woods grabs Carson up and holds him for the fans to see. They rejoice as CJ sends him face first into the outside apron. Carson's skull crashes off the siding and he nearly falls before Woods catches him. ...7! Woods decides to bring the fight back inside and rolls the champion's body under the ropes. CJ follows him in and gets to his feet in the center of the ring. Digital Mortality comes in from behind with a chop block that slows Shaman a bit, but doesn't take him down. Digi stands, only to get cut down by a forearm. Bryan: Oh God, Shaman's asserting himself once again, and he's just laid everyone out! He's the only man standing in the ring now! Yale: This could be it, JB, he could be on his way back to becoming number one contender! Bryan: It's going to take two or three people to get rid of this guy, and I'm not sure, with all the bad blood in the ring, that there are any pacts to be made. Shaman looks for anyone who might be twitching, and sees just that from Valentine. He peels her off the canvas and lifts her into a guerilla press, taking sadistic enjoyment from her helplessness. Bryan: Oh God, Valentine's up here, this could be the end! Yale: No two-time winner this year, JB! If Knight doesn't hold a record, nobody else will! Starting to walk to the ropes, Shaman finds a weak-kneed Clyde Fox standing in his way. Still reeling from the effects of the chokeslam, Clyde seems moments from collapsing, but refuses to budge. Shaman simply grins and throws a boot at Clyde's face without losing Valentine. Clyde ducks and throws his entire body weight behind his shoulder, which he drives straight between Shaman's legs. An amused roar thanks Clyde as Shaman drops Valentine harmlessly to the mat and falls to his knees. Bryan: HEY! That's one way to take Shaman down! Yale: Simple but effective, I guess! Bryan: Shaman's hurting big time here, but can the rest of the crowd capitalize on Clyde's gift! Shaman tries to get back to his feet, but he's hit with a double-axe by Taylor. Shaman swings a big right hand but Taylor dodges the blow, and Axel steps in with a boot to the chest. Taylor and Axel band together and move Shaman to the ropes, where each man takes a leg and begins lifting him out. Bryan: Zacharia Taylor and Axel Action are determined to end Shaman's reign of terror in this match! Finally recovering, Terror stands and moves to help. But Valentine grabs a handful of hair and yanks him down from behind. The crowd cheers as she mounts Terror and begins tearing into him once again. Bryan: Wow, Valentine really hurt Terror there, but I'm not so sure how wise that was! They've got Shaman reeling here, but she wants more of Terror! Yale: She's got a spiteful streak in her, JB, and it's hurting the cause of everyone in the ring right now! I'm telling you, she's not as great as people think she is! Bryan: Valentine wants this match more than anything in the world, she needs the GCW World Title back, but she's also not going to sit back and let Terror be the one to stop her from getting there. Starting to get his wind back, Shaman begins to fight Axel and Taylor off. He kicks Axel away, which allows him to drop to the mat. He then headbutts Taylor, who staggers away and gets cut down by a spinning heel kick from Digital Mortality. Shaman looks down at Axel, who is on all fours in front of him. Bryan: God, Shaman's just not going away, Dave! Yale: And Axel's in his sights! Just as Shaman rights himself, Clyde springs off the ropes and leaps off of Axel's back. He drives his body into Shaman's chest, sending both of them over the top rope! Clyde reaches out and grabs the ropes, and he hangs there as Shaman drops beside him. The big man hits the ground to an enormous pop from the crowd, and Clyde frantically drags himself back in. Bryan: OH MY GOD! There it is! There it is! Clyde eliminates Shaman! I don't believe it! Yale: WOW! Shaman slowly stands as Clyde backs away warily. The officials swarm Shaman to ensure that he can't re-enter the ring, and the big man snarls furiously. Bryan: Shaman just had an absolutely dominant performance here tonight, he eliminated five wrestlers, he lasted a shade under seventy minutes, but his quest to win Dangerous Games was just cut short by Clyde Fox! Yale: The little man strikes again, JB! Clyde's eliminated Shaman and LeStatt Knight tonight, JB! I absolutely cannot believe how well he's done! Bryan: Clyde entered at lucky number thirteen and he's been as good or better as everyone else in the ring! Yale: Meanwhile, Digital Mortality's getting in another gear... With Taylor pinned against the corner, Digi lays furious boots into his gut. He then pulls Taylor out and Digi whips him hard across the ring, following a half step behind. Taylor hits the turnbuckle and gets slammed by a vicious clothesline. Bryan: Wow, Digi's opening up on Taylor here! This is getting ugly! Yale: I'm telling you, JB, this whole Man in Black thing has been a prelude to this moment, when Digital Mortality ascends to the top of GCW! Bryan: Digital Mortality would present a hell of a challenge to Murray, that's for sure! But right now he's focused on Zacharia Taylor! Taylor tries to fight back with a right hand, but Digi blocks it with ease and snapmares him out of the corner. Digi hops onto the second rope and dives off with a dropkick straight into the back of Taylor's head. Yale: Wow, Digi's destroying Taylor here! Bryan: Terror's otherwise occupied with Valentine at the moment, and Taylor's the next freshest guy in the ring. Digital Mortality wants to wear him down and take him out! Taylor slowly picks himself up, but Digi promptly applies a cobra clutch from behind. The fans gasp as Digi attempts the Digitalizer, but Taylor counters by dropping to a knee and throwing Digi off. Digi rolls across the ring and hops to his feet, but Axel explodes into him with a huge clothesline. Digi flips over the top rope but lands upright on the apron, where he grabs Axel with a front facelock. Bryan: WHOA! Counter by Taylor, Digital Mortality was almost tossed there! But he's got Axel hooked up! Yale: Something's gotta give here, JB! Digi lifts Axel off his feet and into a vertical suplex. Axel hovers over the floor, but gets a hand on the top rope in an effort to keep himself steady. With a twist he manages to drop onto the turnbuckle opposite Digi, who begins to climb to negate the high ground. Bryan: Whoa, Axel counters, they're going up! This is dangerous, Dave! Yale: Someone's going down here, it's just a matter of who! Axel and Digi are squaring off! Axel sticks Digi with a forearm to slow him down, then rehooks him for the suplex. Axel manages to lift him off the turnbuckle, but Digi slides down his back and lands seated on the apron. He floats Axel through, ripping him off his foothold and delivering a powerbomb off the turnbuckle. Axel hits the mat with incredible force and doesn't move again. Bryan: OH MY GOD! Yale: WHAT A COUNTER! Bryan: Digital Mortality landed a floatover powerbomb and just eliminated Axel Action! That was a sensational counter! Axel entered at number two and damn near went the distance, but Digital Mortality's turned it on big time and he might just be the favorite to take this match! Digi stands and glances down at Axel before turning to the ring. But Taylor throws himself at Digi, delivering a huge shoulder block with his last gasp of strength. He knocks Digi off his feet, but Digi hangs on, clinging to the top rope and hanging just above elimination. Yale: Whoa! Digi's almost gone! Bryan: Taylor came out of nowhere and knocked him off the apron! But Digi's not gone yet! Taylor pulls himself up next to Digi and tries to pry his fingers apart, but Digi won't let go. Taylor finally opens his mouth and sinks his teeth into Digi's fingers. A trickle of blood leaks out as Digi's grip falters, and he drops to the floor next to Axel. Bryan: THERE IT IS! Yale: Oh my God! That was vicious, JB! Bryan: Zacharia Taylor did what was necessary to get rid of Digital Mortality! I don't believe it! Yale: Digi was taking control, and he absolutely brutalized Taylor for a moment there! But Taylor managed to better him in the end, with no small amount of help from Axel! Bryan: And we're down to the Final Four of 2009! It's Valentine, it's Terror, it's Taylor and it's Fox! Yale: History says one of these wrestlers is the next GCW World Champion, JB! And if it's Clyde Fox I'll quit right here tonight! Bryan: Clyde has stepped it up here tonight, and he's showing that he's got what it takes to hang with the best of the best! What a showing he's had here tonight! Yale: But he's got no chance, JB, because Terror's going to take this match! I can feel it! Bryan: The crowd seems to be getting behind Valentine here! They want to see her become the first ever two-time winner! Yale: Or this could be the night Zacharia Taylor takes the next step... But he's got to go through Terror! Bryan: What a shocker this is. How despicable it is that Terror has apparently aligned himself with President Caldera, even after all that's happened. Yale: Just drop it, JB, we'll deal with that later, right now it's about the Final Four at Dangerous Games! We're close to a winner! Valentine, the clear veteran of the group, begins to take charge. Despite her fatigue, she begins calling over to Fox, slowly making her way to his corner. Clyde seems cautious but receptive, nodding along to Valentine's scheming. Taylor remains stoic in his corner, while Terror, sensing trouble, stiffens. Bryan: And now Vivica's directing traffic here, and you've got to believe she's got a huge advantage here! She's been in this situation several times before, and her experience may give her just the edge she needs to win this thing again. Yale: She's been in there longest of everyone, though, JB, and if they were smart they'd target her instead of anyone else! Bryan: Well, Valentine and Fox have been in there longer than anyone, so it makes sense that they're apparently banding together here to take on Terror, who has a massive edge having entered at number thirty! Snarling, Terror beckons his adversaries forward, and Valentine and Clyde oblige. Rushing forward they swarm Terror in the corner, earning a massive roar from the crowd as they begin to pummel him. Terror fights violently, but Fox and Valentine overwhelm him. While Fox boots Terror in the gut, Valentine begins hoisting him off his feet. For his part Taylor simply remains in his corner, watching idly. Bryan: And here goes Terror! Valentine and Fox are trying to work him out of the ring, he may be done here! Some brilliant quarterbacking here by Valentine! Yale: But the smartest man in the ring is Taylor! He's just going to sit back and let these three people wear each other out while he takes a breather. Taylor continues to struggle mightily, finally managing to elbow Fox in the face. Fox stumbles out of the corner, at which points Taylor pounces. He explodes forward with a devastating clothesline, sending Fox rocketing into the mat. The fans gasp at the impact, while Taylor, not missing a beat, drives a vicious boot into the midsection of Valentine. Valentine stumbles away, but rather than pursuing her Taylor grabs Terror and keeps the pressure on. Taylor begins to overpower Terror, muscling him over the top rope. The crowd thunders as Terror topples out of the ring, but lands safely on the apron and wraps his arms around the bottom rope. Bryan: Whoa, out goes Terror, but he just seems like he's in complete control here. Yale: He hasn't missed a beat, JB, he looks like he's in the best shape of his career. This could be a legendary night for "The Established" Jay Terror! Bryan: But that was some devastating power from Zacharia Taylor there, Clyde Fox must feel like Henry the fifth! Yale: Huh? Bryan: Decapitated. Yale: I knew that. Bryan: Taylor had a great draw at 26, he's still got some scary explosiveness, and that could be the difference here. Seeing that Terror is firmly clinging to the ropes, Taylor breaks off and moves for Valentine. Valentine meets him with a right hand, but Taylor shrugs it off and delivers a big forearm. Valentine stumbles back into the corner, and Taylor rushes in full speed, latching on before Valentine can regain her balance. Taylor begins lifting Valentine toward the top rope, and the crowd rises in fear. A cheer wells up as Fox gets to his feet and charges into the corner, but Taylor senses him coming, drops Valentine, and rushes right into Clyde with a brutal spear. Clyde hits the mat hard, and Taylor goes right back to work on Valentine. Bryan: Whoa, Zacharia Taylor is absolutely taking over this Final Four! That's twice now he's about obliterated Clyde Fox, and he's got Valentine in peril on the ropes! Yale: This crowd is starting to turn on Taylor a bit, too, JB. Just goes to show you these people don't understand the stakes! This match can make a career, make a legacy, and even I know Taylor's just doing what he's got to do! Bryan: I think the fans know it, too, Dave, but they came here hoping to see Valentine win her second Dangerous Games match, and right now that's looking doubtful. Taylor manages to hoist Valentine onto the top rope, but she underhooks the rope with her leg and won't move any further. Frustrated, Taylor steps back and delivers a hard kick to the ribs, causing Valentine to bounce high into the air and out of the ring! The fans gasp but Valentine is able to land on her feet on the apron. Wasting no time, Taylor springs into the ropes to charge Valentine. But from the corner Clyde Fox pulls down the top rope, low-bridging Taylor and sending him flying out of the ring. He lands hard on his head on the floor, earning a huge roar from the crowd and a dazed grin from Fox. Bryan: WHOA! Taylor's gone! Yale: Haha, that was actually a great, sneaky move from Clyde there! Bryan: Taylor was on the brink of eliminating Vivica J. Valentine and making huge waves in the process, but Clyde Fox dropped the top and Taylor spilled out of the ring! We're down to three, and the most dominant force left in this match has been eliminated! Nearing the end, the crowd begins a wild, constant cheer. Valentine drops back into the ring, while Fox, still dazed from Taylor's attack, remains on the mat. Seeing an opening, Terror charges at Valentine, wrapping her up and shoving her into the ropes. The two trade tight right hands, struggling fiercely until they both crash right out of the ring! The crowd explodes, but quickly realizes they have fallen out through the ropes. Yale: Whoa, for a second there I thought Clyde Fox had won Dangerous Games! That was close! Bryan: Valentine and Terror went out through the ropes there, they're still alive. Both wrestlers get to their feet, and Terror drills Valentine with a right hand. He begins ripping into Valentine, backing her into the apron. But Valentine suddenly lowers her shoulder and bulrushes Terror right into the announce table. She drives him right over the table and the two crash into the floor, sending Bryan and Yale scattering. Bryan: Whoa, watch out! Yale: Oh no! Come on, Jay! Don't let her push you around! Bryan: There's some serious bad blood here folks! The last time these two met, Jay Terror ended Valentine's World Title reign! And Valentine has a long memory! The two continue to struggle behind the announce table as the crowd goes wild. Valentine takes control, pulling Terror up and drilling him with a forearm. Terror struggles away from the table, heading back to the ring. Valentine gives chase, catching up to Terror on the apron and preventing him from reentering the ring. Bryan: Valentine is incensed here! She wants to take the fight right to the fresh man in the match, she wants to eliminate Terror from Dangerous Games and end his World Title hopes! Yale: They've got to watch out for Clyde here, JB... Bryan: I think Valentine's done this enough times to know exactly where everyone is at any time! And Fox is still recovering! Yale: He's taken some serious punishment in this match, JB, it's amazing that he's still alive! Terror boots Valentine in the gut to cut off her attack. He drills her with a right hand, but Valentine returns fire to the elation of the crowd. The two exchange shots as the volume of the reaction increases, until Terror drops Valentine with a kick to the knee. The crowd snarls as Terror turns to enter the ring, but Fox is on his feet and sprinting to the turnbuckle. Terror has no time to react as Fox's lightning-quick springboard off the second turnbuckle precedes a huge dropkick to the face. Terror is sent careening off the apron with such velocity that he flies directly into the announce table, which splinters on impact. The crowd explodes as the announcers try to collect themselves once again, and Fox is left standing tall in the ring. Bryan: WHOA! Oh my God! "HO-LY SHIT! HO-LY SHIT!" comes the chant from the crowd as the officials surround Terror on the floor. Inside the ring, Valentine slowly stands and glances down with a look of satisfaction. She then turns her focus to Clyde, who, realizing he is the only one left in the ring, begins backing away to the corner. Bryan: My God, Clyde Fox just sent Jay Terror into the announce table! Terror's been destroyed here! Yale: Oh no! What the hell?! Where did that come from?! Bryan: I think Jay Terror's just been taken right out of this match! We're down to just Clyde and Vivica here! Yale: No way, is Jay really out? Bryan: It sure looks like it! Caldera's guarantee falls flat once again! Yale: Damnit! Jay's comeback was almost a huge one, but he just fell short! Things just can't seem to go right for The Establishment this year! The fans rise to their feet in anticipation of the final two. Valentine offers a sympathetic but unyielding look at Clyde, who nods and marches forward. Bryan: We're down to just Valentine and Fox here! Valentine came in at eleven, Clyde at thirteen, they're just about even on all fronts here! This place has gone absolutely crazy! Yale: Valentine's one elimination away from becoming the first ever two-time Dangerous Games winner, JB! And I'd actually rather see that happen than see Clyde Fox win this thing! Bryan: I don't think Clyde's going to back down, but Valentine wants this win, she needs this win! The two lock up in the center of the ring, but Valentine quickly seizes control. Rushing Clyde into the ropes, she opens with an Irish whip. Clyde bounces back and can't avoid a high back elbow from Valentine. Dazed, Clyde quickly stands up, but Valentine puts him right back down with a dropkick. Bryan: Oh man, and Valentine's wearing Clyde out here! Clyde's had a hell of a performance, he's really risen fast lately, but against the surefire Hall of Famer he might be in a mismatch! Yale: Clyde's presence here is a fluke, JB, nothing more! Get him out of here so we can have a legit Dangerous Games winner, even if it is Valentine! The crowd roars for Valentine's offensive, but a smattering of pro-Clyde boos noticeably follows. The small but audible negativity causes Valentine to briefly pause, allowing Clyde to reach his feet. Valentine very quickly snaps back into action, rushing Clyde back to the corner and lifting him onto the top turnbuckle. Clyde holds onto the top ropes tightly, but his grip is loosened by a wicked knife-edge chop to the chest. Bryan: It may be a short stay here for Clyde in the final two! Valentine looks like she's not taking any prisoners here! Yale: Poor Clyde's run into someone who simply refuses to lose, JB! It was a nice run, but he's got no shot here! Valentine climbs the turnbuckle in front of Clyde and applies a front facelock. She begins pulling Clyde off his seat, and the crowd roars as Fox begins teetering toward the floor. Bryan: And here goes Clyde! Valentine's going to lift him right out of the ring! With a sharp yell, Valentine overpowers Clyde and lifts him up for a suplex. The fans thunder as she leans forward and drops him to the floor. But Clyde curls back in and lands on the turnbuckle again, where he promptly counters Valentine into a suplex of his own! Barely able to hold her steady, Clyde simply lets go, dumping Valentine out of the ring. Bryan: OH MY GOD! THERE GOES VALENTINE! She flies headfirst to the floor, but somehow manages to hook the top rope between her legs. The rope sags and she falls within inches of the ground, but referee Hal Jenkins is in perfect position to see that not even her hair touches the ground. On the rebound she flips herself back up and lands on the apron, which an exhausted Clyde drops back to the mat. Yale: WHOA! I thought he had her for a second there! Bryan: Valentine just came within nanometers of hitting the ground, but that was some truly uncanny agility displayed there! Somehow Valentine is still alive, and Clyde can't believe it! Yale: Fox was literally within a hair of going to NC-17, JB, but I think GCW just got seriously bailed out! Can you imagine Clyde Fox wrestling for the GCW Title?! Bryan: As a matter of fact I can, but for the moment he's looking like he might be completely out of gas. The two finalists reach their feet at the same time, and again Valentine is the aggressor. She drives three stiff forearms into the face of Fox, rocking his head back with each blow. She then sends him into the ropes, and Clyde bounces back ducking a clothesline. On the second bounce Valentine leaps into the air, landing right on Clyde's shoulders. But before she can attempt the hurricanrana, Clyde shoves her back off to the mat, where he immediately flows down into a DDT! The crowd erupts in shock as Valentine's skull bounces off the canvas and doesn't move again. Yale: What a counter! I don't believe it! Bryan: Perhaps I spoke too soon, Clyde just countered the hurricanrana into a brutal DDT! With one shot he may have just opened the door to a huge new step in his career! Yale: Valentine never saw it coming, that was a great reversal, but now he's got to find a way to get her over the top rope. I'm not sure he's got the strength left to do the job! Bryan: Clyde certainly has left it all in the ring tonight, but if he can just muster one more big gasp, he'll have Dangerous Games 2009 in his pocket! A decidedly emphatic chant of "CLYDE! CLYDE! CLYDE!" goes up in the stadium, fueling Fox to his feet. A woozy Valentine gets to her knees, where she absorbs a right hand to the face. She defiantly stands, but can't answer as Clyde drills her with a European uppercut. Valentine staggers back against the ropes and Clyde, seeing his opening, charges forward. Bryan: HERE IT COMES! Unleashing a wild clothesline, Fox swings for the fences. But Valentine regains her bearings just in time to duck and counter with a back body drop! The fans explode as Fox flies out of the ring, but he grabs the top rope and nimbly lands on the apron! Yale: NO! Fox was almost gone, but he held on! Bryan: But Valentine's got him out of the ring! Knowing Fox is inches from elimination, Valentine spins and throws a violent right hand. Clyde counters by ducking between the ropes and driving his shoulder into Valentine's gut. Staggering away, Valentine is left vulnerable, and Clyde promptly leaps cleanly to the top rope! He throws himself at Valentine, connecting cleanly with a big cross body. Bryan: BIG MOVE FROM CLYDE! Yale: ...BUT VALENTINE CAUGHT HIM! The astonished crowd roars as Valentine flies backwards, still on her feet with Clyde in her arms. She manages to keep her balance until she backs into the ropes, where she wrenches Clyde over and tosses him away! Clyde grasps for anything within reach, but the force of Valentine's throw is too much. Clyde flies to the floor and hits with a thud, which is immediately followed by an earsplitting pop from a crowd that has witnessed a legendary moment. Bryan: THERE GOES CLYDE! OH MY GOD! VALENTINE HAS WON IT! Yale: TWO TIMES, JB! TWO TIMES! Bryan: VIVICA J. VALENTINE HAS WON DANGEROUS GAMES! The crowd unleashes a massive cheer for Valentine, even as some Fox fans express their disappointment. But Valentine barely reacts, a sudden look of realization on her face. She turns, but has no time to celebrate her second Dangerous Games victory. Bryan: Oh God, wait a minute! Yale: Could it be?! Less than a second after Clyde hit the ground, Valentine is greeted by a charging Jay Terror. With incredible force the two collide, and a Terror clothesline sends Valentine flipping wildly out of the ring. Her body smacks off the apron and drops to the ground, landing in a heap next to Fox. The stadium goes silent for a moment before exploding in shock, as Jay Terror is left on his knees in the ring. Bryan: WHAT JUST HAPPENED?! Yale: Valentine's gone, JB! I don't believe it! Terror just threw Valentine out! Bryan: But I thought... oh, wait just a damn minute! Yale: YES! YES! I think they're giving the match to Terror, JB! Lead official Josh Briggs, looking dismayed, reluctantly enters the ring and raises Jay Terror's arm. The bell sounds and Terror's music cues up once again, confirming the unthinkable. Bryan: NO! DAMNIT, NO! JAY TERROR HAS WON DANGEROUS GAMES! Yale: HAHAHA! YES! THIS IS UNBELIEVABLE! Bryan: I sure as hell can't believe it! We thought Terror had been eliminated by Fox, but I don't think he ever reentered the ring! He was still alive all this time! Yale: And if you look at the replay, I think Valentine knew it, JB! She didn't react when she beat Clyde, because Terror was lying in wait that whole time! The crowd boos furiously as Terror stands tall, throwing his arms into the air in the center of the ring. Andrews: Here is your winner of the 2009 Dangerous Games. . . "THE ESTABLISHED" JAAAAY TERRROR!!!!!!!! Bryan: NO! Damnit, no! I stood here and watched as Jay slinked away from our announce table, but he was just waiting for Valentine to turn her back! Valentine eliminated Fox and an instant later, Terror sprung and ROBBED Valentine of a second Dangerous Games win! Yale: She came so close, JB, but Terror may have outfoxed her tonight! And just like that, GCW will have the man they NEEDED in the main event at NC-17! What a shocker! Bryan: I'll say! Of all the speculation and all the conjecture over the last few weeks, nobody ever imagined that Jay Terror could return and shock the wrestling world by winning Dangerous Games in his first match back! And for that matter, that he could count of President Caldera for the huge assist in giving him number thirty! Yale: Terror and Caldera have reconciled, JB, and the result is a better than ever Jay Terror! What a moment! What a win! I don't think I've ever been happier! The crowd expresses its feelings to the contrary, relentlessly booing the decision, pounding Terror with hatred as he smirks in the center of the ring. Outside, a disappointed Fox sits with his knees pressed against his chest, leaning against the guardrail in defeat. Not far away, a shocked and incensed Valentine has cornered Jenkins and Vale, screaming furiously with eyes full of rage. Bryan: My God, Valentine has lost it here! She cannot believe what just happened! She was so damn close! In my opinion she got robbed! Yale: Rules are rules, JB! Terror didn't reenter the ring and he was never eliminated! Bryan: He was down for a long time, there ought to be some rule about that! He can't just wait outside of the ring until the last person's back is turned! It was a sneaky move and he's robbed both Valentine and Fox because of it! Yale: Don't blame Terror for being smarter than everyone else, JB! Bryan: Valentine and Fox just fought their hearts out, and one of them deserves to be going to NC-17 to face Andy Murray! But instead it's this sell-out son of a bitch, who obviously still doesn't have enough spine to stand up to President Caldera! Yale: And why should he?! They had their falling out, but they've obviously reconnected and it's resulted in one of the biggest wins of Terror's career! Terror's the number one contender once again, JB! Bryan: Look, Terror was great in this match, and he looks like he's obviously in the best shape of his life! But this is not at all how this match should have gone down! Of all the Dangerous Games matches I've called, not since Shane Lane have I been so disgusted with the outcome! Yale: Whoa, watch it, JB, don't say something you're gonna regret... Terror mounts the turnbuckle, staring down mockingly at Valentine and Fox. Valentine attempts to climb back into the ring to get at Terror, but the officials cut her off and refuse her entry. The boos don't let up as the crowd continues to let Terror know just exactly what they think of him. Bryan: This is an atrocity. I really thought, after all we'd seen and heard, that Jay Terror was going to get back to being the Outlaw we all loved. But he just can't get out from under Caldera's spell, and this horrible display is what we get out of Dangerous Games 2009! Yale: What spell? Terror's career didn't take off until he started studying with Caldera, and now he's won Dangerous Games! You're seeing the start of a new dawn in the life of Jay Terror, and Valentine's just going to have to wait another year! Bryan: Well, Caldera said his goal was to have the right guy in the most important match at NC-17, and it looks like, from his perspective, he's got his wish! Jay Terror has won Dangerous Games whether we like it or not! As Fox somberly exits the ring area, Valentine is herded toward the ramp. Terror remains on the turnbuckle, waving goodbye, mocking both Valentine and the crowd in grotesque celebration. Bryan: Fans, I sincerely apologize for this night. Jay Terror has just robbed Vivica J. Valentine of another Dangerous Games victory and another shot at the GCW World Championship. Mercifully we're out of time. For David Yale, this is James Bryan saying goodnight from Dangerous Games 2009! The show fades to a close with Jay Terror, still Established, standing tall as the new number one contender, the right man for the right match at NC-17...
